# Review — 075-agents-md-holds-what-every-session-needs

## Summary

075's first review. The window runs from `cc25ee6e` to HEAD. One agent ran all five passes, reading all nine loaded backend and cross rule files whole. It read 19 of the 47 scope entries whole: 075's spec, plan and tasks, AGENTS.md and the constitution, the four project templates, the shipped hook, the prune command, its reference and help, runtime-tools.txt, prune_project.rs, registry.rs, gen-help-tables.sh (a plan entry unchanged in the window), the prune-project mutation list and the resolve-constitutions size test. In depth it read the hunks no committed review had read: `b763977c`, `cb30b333`, `bfa85e3b`, `cfab0759`, the shared `2f61d67d`, `ab5eaa49` and `73f21fc9` (041's and 050's reviews read only what changed after this one), and the two commits naming no spec, `2604a6f9`'s AGENTS.md hunk and `b1d319f1`'s primitives.rs hunk. The other entries are unread: the bootstrap, README, docs/analyze.md, configure, target, check_artifact_size.rs, resolve_constitutions.rs and the runtime's interpreter, main, server, primitives mod and schema files were read at those hunks and what each criterion needs; govern.md was checked byte-identical to ductus.md and the four generated copies against their sources; docs/shared-constitution.md, config_keys.rs and the resolve-constitutions mutation list carry only `c6a0dca2`, which 055's review read. Two findings were fixed in the run: prune-project's result is bounded past `[artifacts] read-size-bytes` (BE-PAYLOAD-001), and a file ending inside an open comment or managed region names its unexamined tail (QUAL-CLAIM-001). Of the observations, the stale hermetic claim in 026's AC15 and its Family 22 scenario was corrected in 026 on the owner's decision, the audit header and reference, the docs sweep, the UTF-8 hoist and the shared project-file list were fixed, and two were waived by rule.

1 fix round(s): 1 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

## Observations

- bug — Family 22's ticked claim that the runtime is stubbed no longer holds: cfab0759's project-file growth cases drive the real runtime and the family now needs a reachable binary, so it is no longer hermetic. Spec 026 AC15 ('the runtime is stubbed so the family stays hermetic') and its scenario family-22-adopter-shell-behavior ('The runtime is a stub ... the family stays hermetic: no cargo build') describe the earlier shape. 026 is done and is not in 075's cross-spec-impact, so no obligation records it. — `specs/026-framework-self-audit/spec.md:139` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed**
- convention — two live artifacts still state the family is hermetic or stubbed: the header comment of scripts/audit/adopter-shell-behavior.sh and step 22 of framework/reference/audit.md (its installed copy .ductus/reference/audit.md follows by the generator). cfab0759 updated scripts/audit/README.md only. Mechanical prose sweep, no requirement added. Checked by grep for 'stubbed'/'hermetic' across the live-artifact set: these two plus the 026 spec and scenario (see the entry above). — `scripts/audit/adopter-shell-behavior.sh:34` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — the sweep behind AC10 left docs/slash-commands.md and README.md stale. The /prune section still routes a plan section's contributor knowledge to 'AGENTS.md when it holds for this project alone', the default that framework/commands/prune.md step 8 no longer applies (it sends knowledge to AGENTS.md only when every session needs it), and neither the section nor README.md's /prune bullet mentions --project, which walks no spec and so also sits outside the 'single-spec scope' the Refine introduction states for /prune. Checked by reading docs/slash-commands.md lines 49 and 61-65 and README.md line 103 against framework/commands/prune.md. — `docs/slash-commands.md:63` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- perf — `search` re-validates a file's whole content as UTF-8 once per catalog identifier: `contains` calls `std::str::from_utf8(hay)` on every call, and `search` calls it for every (tracked file, identifier) pair, so validation work is files x identifiers x bytes where files x bytes is enough. Derived from reading search and contains in prune_project.rs; not timed, since nothing was run past the fixture previews. — `runtime/src/primitives/prune_project.rs:516-549` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — the list of the four project files exists twice in runtime code: `prune_project::subjects` (lines 48-56) and `check_artifact_size::project_subjects` (lines 298-307) build the identical array, and the latter's doc comment says 'prune-project walks the same four'. A fifth file or a rename edited in one place leaves the size check and the walk measuring different sets. Reuse, not a rule violation: both already share ProjectFileKind. — `runtime/src/primitives/prune_project.rs:48` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — 73f21fc9's cut of the constitution's Frontmatter Schema sentence removed the enumeration '(`system.md`, `errors.md`, `events.md`, `inbox.md`, plan, tasks, rule and README files)' that spec 013's ticked AC3 repeats in its own parenthetical. The constitution still declares that other artifacts MAY carry frontmatter, so AC3's claim holds; only its paraphrased list names artifacts the constitution no longer lists. Checked by grep for the old sentence across the live artifacts: one referrer, specs/013-text-first-artifacts/spec.md. — `specs/013-text-first-artifacts/spec.md:111` — **waived**: The criterion's requirement still holds against the constitution's current text; correcting the parenthetical edits a criterion of the done spec 013, a reopen with nothing to buy, and the constitution's own 050 task 44 made the cut. The finding lands inside a closed spec outside any plan, so it is waived with this reason rather than parked. (decided by the rule)
- convention — 2604a6f9's two Gotchas are claims that check out against the tree (be900a92 is a docs/ commit naming no spec; spec 081 says every path outside the spec root, .ductus/, AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md is code; the Write tool refuses an overwrite of an unread file), and AGENTS.md measures 49,767 bytes, under scripts/lint-governing-size.sh's 50,000 ceiling (lint run clean). Against 075's own four-part test, the subagent-Write trap is true of every project that fans work out to subagents, which §drift-prevention routes upstream, and the docs/ rule is partly stated by spec 081 and by the runtime-currency bullet beside it. — `AGENTS.md:86` — **waived**: Both sit beside five sibling subagent and review-cost entries filed under the same house convention, the file is within its ceiling, and routing any of them upstream or to spec 081 is the owner's call, not a defect in the commit. (decided by the rule)
